aQR8TWgkQaaankgxa3MwqRCUwJkBk23S2UEA2n8bCOJdNRWL3mFYXNdA2nVWZaB0nkkSnpkW3AZihqlVmkZBky0wTHt3Lm0uCkiWGv9RMoblsTWqzPSx2rQgPZrz7qZ01ntBXKqUZTVsn1/F85xTMXjSyYzRdlzhGmgPpSb2wh27a7DbCaxRLl+z+p9gAQxH52LEU4y/rvJBwBBWC+uC/0p83/zWe8BPaAH9IDvDHhQfgFzgR/WTbo3OQAAAABJRU5ErkJggg=="}</script>
<script type="text/plain" id="codeSrc">"""Download the 11 S&P 500 sector ETFs plus SPY for the Sector Galaxy (RRG) page.

Setup:  pip install yfinance pandas
Run:    python download_sector_etfs.py
Output: sector_etfs.json  ->  DATA LINK in the page -> Load sector file
Prices are dividend-adjusted closes (total return), like the other files.
"""
import json

import pandas as pd
import yfinance as yf

START = "2016-01-01"   # a year of warm-up before the page's 2017 start
TICKERS = ["XLK", "XLF", "XLV", "XLY", "XLP", "XLE", "XLI", "XLB", "XLU", "XLRE", "XLC", "SPY"]

df = yf.download(TICKERS, start=START, auto_adjust=True, progress=False)["Close"]
df.index = pd.DatetimeIndex(df.index).tz_localize(None)
df = df.dropna(subset=["SPY"]).sort_index()   # keep every day SPY traded; younger ETFs start later (XLRE 2015, XLC 2018)

series = {t: [None if pd.isna(v) else round(float(v), 4) for v in df[t]] for t in TICKERS if t in df.columns}
with open("sector_etfs.json", "w") as f:
    json.dump({"source": "yfinance", "dates": [d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") for d in df.index], "series": series}, f, separators=(",", ":"))
print(f"Saved sector_etfs.json: {len(series)} tickers, {len(df)} days, {df.index[0].date()} to {df.index[-1].date()}")
</script>
<script type="text/plain" id="codeSrc2">"""
download_sector_holdings.py  ->  sector_holdings.json

Downloads what is INSIDE each Select Sector SPDR ETF (XLK, XLF, XLV, XLY, XLP, XLE,
XLI, XLB, XLU, XLRE, XLC): every holding, its weight, and its weekly closing prices.
Load the result in Sector Galaxy (DATA panel > "Load holdings file") and every planet
gets a surface: a civilization / ecosystem where each company is a settlement.

Setup (once):   pip install yfinance pandas requests openpyxl lxml
Run:            python download_sector_holdings.py
Output:         sector_holdings.json  (a few MB; takes a few minutes)

Where the holdings come from, in order of preference:
  1. State Street (SSGA) daily holdings file for each ETF  (official, full list, real weights)
  2. Fallback: the S&P 500 members of that GICS sector (from Wikipedia), weighted by market cap.
     (The Select Sector SPDRs hold exactly the S&P 500 members of each sector, so only the
      weights are approximate.)
The file records which source each sector used, and Sector Galaxy shows it.
"""
import io, json, re, sys, datetime as dt
import pandas as pd, requests, yfinance as yf

ETFS = {"XLK": "Information Technology", "XLF": "Financials", "XLV": "Health Care",
        "XLY": "Consumer Discretionary", "XLP": "Consumer Staples", "XLE": "Energy",
        "XLI": "Industrials", "XLB": "Materials", "XLU": "Utilities",
        "XLRE": "Real Estate", "XLC": "Communication Services"}
START = "2017-01-01"
HDR = {"User-Agent": "Mozilla/5.0 (sector-galaxy holdings downloader)"}
SSGA = "https://www.ssga.com/library-content/products/fund-data/etfs/us/holdings-daily-us-en-{}.xlsx"
TICK = re.compile(r"^[A-Z]{1,5}([.\-][A-Z])?$")

def yf_symbol(t):                       # BRK.B -> BRK-B for Yahoo
    return t.replace(".", "-")

def from_ssga(etf):
    r = requests.get(SSGA.format(etf.lower()), headers=HDR, timeout=40); r.raise_for_status()
    raw = pd.read_excel(io.BytesIO(r.content), header=None)
    hdr = next(i for i in range(min(30, len(raw))) if str(raw.iloc[i, 0]).strip().lower() == "name")
    df = pd.read_excel(io.BytesIO(r.content), header=hdr)
    df.columns = [str(c).strip().lower() for c in df.columns]
    tcol = next(c for c in df.columns if "ticker" in c or "identifier" in c)
    wcol = next(c for c in df.columns if "weight" in c)
    out = []
    for _, row in df.iterrows():
        t = str(row[tcol]).strip().upper(); w = pd.to_numeric(row[wcol], errors="coerce")
        if TICK.match(t) and w == w and w > 0: out.append({"t": t, "n": str(row["name"]).strip(), "w": float(w)})
    tot = sum(x["w"] for x in out)
    if len(out) < 8 or tot <= 0: raise ValueError("SSGA file looked empty")
    for x in out: x["w"] = x["w"] / tot
    return out, "SSGA daily holdings"

_wiki = None
def from_wikipedia(etf):
    global _wiki
    if _wiki is None:
        html = requests.get("https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/List_of_S%26P_500_companies", headers=HDR, timeout=40).text
        _wiki = pd.read_html(io.StringIO(html))[0]
    d = _wiki[_wiki["GICS Sector"] == ETFS[etf]]
    rows = []
    for _, r in d.iterrows():
        t = str(r["Symbol"]).strip().upper()
        try: cap = yf.Ticker(yf_symbol(t)).fast_info["market_cap"]
        except Exception: cap = None
        if cap: rows.append({"t": t, "n": str(r["Security"]), "w": float(cap)})
    tot = sum(x["w"] for x in rows)
    for x in rows: x["w"] /= tot
    return rows, "S&P 500 sector members, market-cap weights (approximate)"

holdings, how = {}, {}
for etf in ETFS:
    try: holdings[etf], how[etf] = from_ssga(etf)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"  {etf}: SSGA failed ({str(e)[:70]}), falling back to Wikipedia + market caps")
        holdings[etf], how[etf] = from_wikipedia(etf)
    print(f"{etf}: {len(holdings[etf])} holdings  [{how[etf]}]")

symbols = sorted({x["t"] for h in holdings.values() for x in h})
print(f"downloading prices for {len(symbols)} stocks ...")
px = yf.download([yf_symbol(s) for s in symbols], start=START, auto_adjust=True, progress=True, threads=True)["Close"]
px.columns = [c.replace("-", ".") if c.replace("-", ".") in symbols else c for c in px.columns]
px = px.dropna(how="all")
last_days = px.index.to_series().groupby(px.index.to_period("W")).max()      # last trading day of each week
wk = px.loc[last_days.values]
dates = [d.strftime("%Y-%m-%d") for d in wk.index]

out = {"version": 1, "source": "Sector Galaxy holdings download", "asOf": dates[-1], "dates": dates, "sectors": {}, "how": how}
for etf, hs in holdings.items():
    rows = []
    for x in sorted(hs, key=lambda x: -x["w"]):
        if x["t"] not in wk.columns: continue
        s = wk[x["t"]]
        rows.append({"t": x["t"], "n": x["n"], "w": round(x["w"], 5),
                     "p": [None if pd.isna(v) else round(float(v), 2) for v in s]})
    out["sectors"][etf] = rows
with open("sector_holdings.json", "w") as f: json.dump(out, f, separators=(",", ":"))
print("wrote sector_holdings.json:", sum(len(v) for v in out["sectors"].values()), "holdings,", len(dates), "weeks")
</script>
